								function t3113:_StartTracking()

								function t3113:_StopTracking()

									if _descendantAddedConnection3 == nil then
										return

									self._descendantAddedConnection = nil
									_descendantAddedConnection3:Disconnect()
									local _hooksBySound5 = self._hooksBySound
									local v10292 = nil
									while true do
										local t3116
										v10292, t3116 = _hooksBySound5(nil, v10292)
										if v10292 == nil then
											break

										t3116.destroying:Disconnect()

									table.clear(self._lastPlayedAt)

								function t3113:_TrackSound(p3991)

										self._hooksBySound[p3991] ~= nil then
										return
									end

									local function f12872()
										self:_OnPlayed(p3991)
									end

									local function f12873()
										self:_ForgetSound(p3991)
									end

									t3117.destroying = p3991.Destroying:Connect(f12873)
									_hooksBySound6[p3991] = t3117
								end

								function t3113:_ForgetSound(p3992)
									local t3118 = self._hooksBySound[p3992]
									if t3118 == nil then
										return

									self._hooksBySound[p3992] = nil
									self._lastPlayedAt[p3992] = nil
									t3118.played:Disconnect()
									t3118.destroying:Disconnect()
								end

								function t3240.DestroyDissipate(p4128, p4129, p4130)
									if not p4129 then
										p4129 = 0.2
									end
									if not p4130 then
										p4130 = 0.5
									end
									local v10685 = os.clock()
									local minTransparency3 = p4128.minTransparency
									local contractFrom5 = p4128.contractFrom
									local contractFrom6 = p4128.contractFrom
									local _parts10 = p4128._parts
									local fadeLength3 = p4128.fadeLength
									local maxRadius3 = p4128.maxRadius
									local minThicknessMultiplier3 = p4128.minThicknessMultiplier
									local v10686 = p4129
									local v10687 = contractFrom6 + 1 / (#_parts10 * fadeLength3)
									local v10688 = p4130

									local function f12980()
										local v10689 = os.clock() - v10685
										p4128.minThicknessMultiplier = minThicknessMultiplier3 + (-2 - minThicknessMultiplier3) * v10689 / v10686
										if v10689 < v10686 * 0.4 then
											p4128.minTransparency = minTransparency3 + (contractFrom5 - minTransparency3) * (v10689 / (v10686 * 0.4))
										elseif v10689 < v10686 then
											local v10690 = (v10689 - v10686 * 0.4) / (v10686 * 0.6)
											p4128.minTransparency = contractFrom5 + (v10687 - contractFrom5) * v10690
											p4128.maxRadius = maxRadius3 * (1 + v10688 * v10690)
											p4128.minRadius = p4128.minRadius + (p4128.maxRadius - p4128.minRadius) * v10690
										else
											if os.clock() - p4128._startT < (p4128.pulseLength + 1) / p4128.pulseSpeed then
												p4128:Destroy()
											end
											if p4128._dissipateConnection then
												p4128._dissipateConnection:Disconnect()
											end
											p4128._dissipateConnection = nil
										end
									end

									p4128._dissipateConnection = v10683.Heartbeat:Connect(f12980)
								end

								t3240._UpdateGeometry = (function(c0)
									return (function(T)
									local U = T[0]
									return function(T, c, z, h, K, p, i, D)
										local y, o = 1 - T.maxTransparency, 1 - T.minTransparency
										local l = T.opacityProfileFunction(z, h, T.pulseSpeed, T.pulseLength, T.fadeLength, y, o)
										local y = T.thickness * K * l
										l = y > 0 and l or 0
										local K = 1 - T.contractFrom
										local o = 1 / (D * T.fadeLength)
										if l > K then
											c.Size = Vector3.new((i - p).Magnitude, y, y)
											c.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt((p + i) * 0.5, i) * U.X_INVERSE
											c.Transparency = 1 - l
											return true
										elseif l > K - o then
											local v = (1 - (l - (K - o)) * D * T.fadeLength) * (z < h * T.pulseSpeed - 0.5 * T.pulseLength and 1 or -1)
											c.Size = Vector3.new((1 - math.abs(v)) * (i - p).Magnitude, y, y)
											c.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(p + (i - p) * (math.max(0, v) + 0.5 * (1 - math.abs(v))), i) * U.X_INVERSE
											c.Transparency = 1 - l
											return true
										end
										c.Transparency = 1
										return false
									end
								end)({[0]=c0})
								end)(v10677)
								t3240._UpdateColor = (function(c0)
									return (function(T)
									local U = T[0]
									return function(T, c, z, h)
										local K = T.color
										if typeof(K) == "Color3" then
											c.Color = K
										else

									end
								end)({[0]=c0})
								end)(v10682)

								function t3240:Fade(p4132)
									local v10693 = os.clock()

									function self.opacityProfileFunction(p4133, _, _, _, _, _, p4134)
										local v10694 = p4133 - (1 - (os.clock() - v10693) / p4132)
										if v10694 <= 0 then
											return p4134
										end
										if v10694 < v10677.FADE_EDGE then
											return p4134 * (1 - v10694 / v10677.FADE_EDGE)
										end
										return 0
									end
								end

								t3240.Update = (function(c0,c1)
									return (function(T)
									local U = T[0]
									local c = T[1]
									return function(T)
										if not T.enabled then
											if not T._partsHidden then
												T._partsHidden = true
												T:_Disable()
											end
											return
										end
										T._partsHidden = false
										local z, h = T.minRadius, T.maxRadius
										local K = T._parts
										local p = #K
										local i = T._ranNum
										local D = T.animationSpeed
										local y = T.frequency
										local o, l = T.minThicknessMultiplier, T.maxThicknessMultiplier
										local v = os.clock() - T._startT
										local a, Y = T.spaceCurveFunction, T.radialProfileFunction
										local g = (T.pulseLength + 1) / T.pulseSpeed
										if v >= g then
											if T.autoDestroy then
												T:Destroy()
											end
											return
										end
										local g, S, P, r = T.attachment0, T.attachment1, T.curveSize0, T.curveSize1
										local H, d, u, V = g.WorldPosition, g.WorldPosition + g.WorldAxis * P, S.WorldPosition - S.WorldAxis * r, S.WorldPosition
										local g = a(0, H, d, u, V)
										local S, P = g, g
										for g = 1, p do
											local r = K[g]
											local K = g / p
											local C, n = (D * -v) + y * 10 * K - 0.2 + i * 4, 5 * ((D * 0.01 * -v) / 10 + y * K) + i * 4
											local i = U(5 * C, 1.5, 1 * n, 0, c.ROLL_OFFSET_LOW) + U(0.5 * C, 1.5, 0.1 * n, 0, c.ROLL_OFFSET_HIGH)
											local D = U(3.4, n, C, z, h) * Y(K)
											local z = U(2.3, n, C, o, l)
											local h = a(K, H, d, u, V)
											local y
											if g ~= p then
												y = (CFrame.new(P, h) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, i) * CFrame.Angles(math.acos(math.clamp(U(n, C, 2.7, c.OFFSET_ANGLE, 1), -1, 1)), 0, 0) * CFrame.new(0, 0, -D)).Position
											else
												y = h
											end
											local U = T:_UpdateGeometry(r, K, v, z, S, y, p)
											if U then
												T:_UpdateColor(r, K, v)
											end
											S, P = y, h
										end
									end
								end)({[0]=c0,[1]=c1})
								end)(v10681, v10677)
